TEMPLATE · STATIC RENDER PROOF

Template execution lab

The shared source is parsed before this page is built. AST mode executes the committed nodes; generated mode lowers those same nodes into host-language renderers. Both modes use the same assign, define and output contract.

Cross-language proofPASS
AST modeIMPLEMENTED
Generated mode5 LANGUAGES
Page validationHTML

03 · NATIVE VALUES

Assign keeps the host instance

A class instance passed through assign stays that instance. The template reads a public field, calls the instance method and calls a separately registered logical class function.

template
<article><h1>{= order.label}</h1><p>{= order.status_label("ready")}</p><p>{= Order::suffix("!")}</p></article>
host assign
class Order { public label = 'Order 12'; public status_label(prefix) { return prefix + ':12'; } }
const assign = { order: new Order() };

Rendered output · 64 bytes · 5f94e0e9fe74…

<article><h1>Order 12</h1><p>ready:12</p><p>done!</p></article>

01 · EVIDENCE

Same bytes from AST mode

Source parsing occurs during the build. Runtime checks load the committed artifacts, apply assign and define, and compare UTF-8 output bytes. Generated mode must produce the same bytes before it can be enabled for a language.

ScenarioBytesSHA-256tsgorustphppython
compiler-coverage351f9e5af2122de…PASSPASSPASSPASSPASS
empty-state78d98cc268d4c4…PASSPASSPASSPASSPASS
html-slot111b1fc22f8d29c…PASSPASSPASSPASSPASS
react-boundary1244c247742a44e…PASSPASSPASSPASSPASS
scope-precedence1777b160d439d3d…PASSPASSPASSPASSPASS

02 · INPUT AND OUTPUT

Small SSR situations

Each card shows the exact assign data, define map, every template file, compiled artifact manifest and final HTML.

Generated compiler coverage

One readable page executes every canonical statement and expression family through AST and generated modes.

expressionsloops and loop metadatainclude scopedefine and block scope

Inputs

assign
{
  "flag": true,
  "dangerous": "&<>\"'",
  "empty_list": [],
  "empty_map": {},
  "page": {
    "title": "Guide"
  },
  "numbers": [
    10,
    20
  ],
  "lookup": {
    "x": "X"
  },
  "rows": [
    {
      "name": "A"
    },
    {
      "name": "B"
    }
  ]
}
define
{
  "layout": "layout.tpl",
  "content": {
    "template": "card.tpl",
    "data": {
      "label": "define"
    }
  }
}
templates · parser ranges
--- card.tpl
<p class="card">{= label}</p>


--- layout.tpl
{: values = [0, ...numbers]}
{: merged = [...lookup, 'z' => 'Z']}
<section>
<h1>{= page.title}</h1>
<p class="escaped">{= dangerous}</p>
<p class="logical">{= flag && 'x'}|{= false || 2}</p>
<p class="empty-truthiness">{= empty_list && flag}|{= empty_map && flag}</p>
<p>{= values[1]}|{= merged['z']}</p>
{? flag && page.title == 'Guide'}<strong>matched</strong>{:}<strong>missed</strong>{/}
<p>{= flag ? 'yes' : 'no'}|{= -1 + 3}|{= '' | default('fallback')}</p>
<ul>
{@ row = rows}
<li>{= row.index_}/{= row.size_}:{= row.name}:{= row.first_}:{= row.last_}</li>
{:}
<li>empty</li>
{/}
</ul>
{+ partial.tpl}
{?# content}<p>defined</p>{:}<p>missing</p>{/}
{# content label:page.title}
</section>


--- partial.tpl
<p class="included">{= values[2]}</p>

function generatedEnv(input) {
  if (input === void 0 || input === null) return { timezone: "Z", now: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3) };
  if (!generatedPlain(input)) throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env is not an object");
  const timezone = ("timezone" in input ? input.timezone : void 0) ?? "Z";
  const now = ("now" in input ? input.now : void 0) ?? Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3);
  if (typeof timezone !== "string") throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env.timezone is not a string");
  if (typeof now !== "number") throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env.now is not a number");
  return { timezone, now };
}

	Key   K
	Value V
}

type OrderedMap[K comparable, V any] struct {
	entries []OrderedEntry[K, V]
}

func NewOrderedMap[K comparable, V any]() OrderedMap[K, V] {
	return OrderedMap[K, V]{}
}

func (m *OrderedMap[K, V]) Set(key K, item V) {
	for index := range m.entries {
		if m.entries[index].Key == key {
			m.entries[index].Value = item
			return
		}
	}
	m.entries = append(m.entries, OrderedEntry[K, V]{key, item})
}

func (m OrderedMap[K, V]) Entries() []OrderedEntry[K, V] {
	return m.entries
}

func (m OrderedMap[K, V]) TemplateValue() value.Value {
	result := value.NewOrderedMap()
	for _, entry := range m.entries {
		result.Set(fmt.Sprint(entry.Key), generatedValue(entry.Value))
	}
	return result
}

func (m *OrderedMap[K, V]) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
	decoder := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
	token, err := decoder.Token()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if token != json.Delim('{') {
		return fmt.Errorf("generated ordered map must be an object")
	}
	m.entries = nil
	for decoder.More() {
		rawKey, err := decoder.Token()
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		keyText, ok := rawKey.(string)
		if !ok {
			return fmt.Errorf("generated ordered map key is not text")
		}
		keyJSON, err := json.Marshal(keyText)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		var key K
		if err := json.Unmarshal(keyJSON, &key); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		var item V
		if err := decoder.Decode(&item); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		m.Set(key, item)
	}
	_, err = decoder.Token()
	return err
}

		env.Now = options.Env.Now
	}
	return env
}

func render_partial_tpl(assign Assign, definitions *Definitions, input Input_partial_tpl, context *render.Context, runtime *render.RuntimeBindings, rootData *value.OrderedMap, scope *render.Scope) {
	frame := render.NewFrame("partial.tpl", errs.LineIndex{0, 38}, rootData)

func renderTemplate(target string, assign Assign, definitions *Definitions, context *render.Context, runtime *render.RuntimeBindings, rootData *value.OrderedMap, scope *render.Scope) {
	switch target {
	case "layout.tpl":
		render_layout_tpl(assign, definitions, Input_layout_tpl{}, context, runtime, rootData, scope)
		return
	default:
		panic(context.Fail(errs.LoadNotFound, nil, nil, "template "+target+" does not exist"))
	}
}

type generatedTarget struct {
	target string
	html   *string
}

func generatedBindDefinitions(input map[string]template.DefineInput) (*Definitions, map[string]generatedTarget, error) {
	definitions := &Definitions{}
	targets := map[string]generatedTarget{}
	for id, input := range input {
		if !utf8.ValidString(id) {
			return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataInvalidUTF8, Message: "a define id is not valid UTF-8"}
		}
		switch id {
		case "content":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				if input.Template != "" || input.Data != nil {
					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
				}
				definitions.Content = &Definition[DefinitionData_card_tpl]{HTML: input.HTML}
				targets[id] = generatedTarget{html: input.HTML}
				continue
			}
			if input.Template != "card.tpl" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[DefinitionData_card_tpl]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				data, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap)
				if !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := DefinitionData_card_tpl{}
				if raw, found := data.Get("label"); found {
					converted, err := generatedAs[string](raw)
					if err != nil {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s.data.%s: %w", id, "label", err)
					}
					definitionData.Label = &converted
				}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Content = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: "card.tpl"}
		case "layout":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
			}
			if input.Template != "layout.tpl" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[DefinitionData_layout_tpl]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if _, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap); !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := DefinitionData_layout_tpl{}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Layout = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: "layout.tpl"}
		default:
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s is not declared", id)
		}
	}
	return definitions, targets, nil
}

type GeneratedProgram struct {
	Runtime *template.RuntimeEnvironment
}

func NewGeneratedProgram(options template.Options) (*GeneratedProgram, error) {
	runtime, err := template.NewRuntimeEnvironment(options.Limits, options.Functions)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &GeneratedProgram{Runtime: runtime}, nil
}

type generatedPrepared struct {
	name        string
	target      string
	assign      Assign
	definitions *Definitions
	rootData    *value.OrderedMap
	env         functions.Env
	runtime     *template.RuntimeEnvironment
	html        *string
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) Render() (string, error) {
	return render.Guard(p.name, p.render)
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) render() (string, error) {
	context := render.NewContext(p.runtime, p.rootData, p.env, p.target)
	runtime := render.NewRuntimeBindings(context)
	scope := render.NewScope()
	if err := context.Enter(p.target, nil, nil); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer context.Leave()
	if p.html != nil {
		if err := context.Write(*p.html, nil, nil); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	} else {
		renderTemplate(p.target, p.assign, p.definitions, context, runtime, p.rootData, scope)
	}
	return context.Output(), nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Prepare(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	name, ok := target.(string)
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("generated target must be a template name")
	}
	return render.Guard(name, func() (template.Prepared, error) {
		return p.prepare(name, assign, options)
	})
}

func generatedBindFailure(name string, err error) error {
	var bindError *value.BindError
	if errors.As(err, &bindError) {
		return errs.WithoutPosition(bindError.Code, name, bindError.Message)
	}
	return err
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) prepare(name string, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	rootData, err := value.BindMap(assign)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	var typedAssign Assign
	if err := generatedDecode(rootData, &typedAssign); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	definitions, targets, err := generatedBindDefinitions(options.Define)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	resolved := targets[name]
	targetName := name
	if resolved.target != "" {
		targetName = resolved.target
	}
	return &generatedPrepared{name: name, target: targetName, assign: typedAssign, definitions: definitions, rootData: rootData, env: generatedEnv(options), runtime: p.Runtime, html: resolved.html}, nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Render(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (string, error) {
	prepared, err := p.Prepare(target, assign, options)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return prepared.Render()
}

var _ template.Program = (*GeneratedProgram)(nil)

fn generated_bind_definitions(name: &str, input: &HashMap<String, polyspec_template::DefineInput>) -> Result<(Definitions, HashMap<String, GeneratedTarget>), RequestError> { let mut definitions = Definitions::default(); let mut targets = HashMap::new(); for (id, input) in input { match id.as_str() {

            "layout" => { if let Some(html) = &input.html { if true || input.template.is_some() || input.data.is_some() { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid html entry"))); } definitions.layout = Some(Definition { html: Some(html.clone()), ..Default::default() }); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: None, html: Some(html.clone()) }); } else { if input.template.as_deref() != Some("layout.tpl") { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid template"))); }

func generatedPlain(input any) any {
	switch item := input.(type) {
	case *value.OrderedMap:
		result := map[string]any{}

			result[index] = generatedPlain(entry)
		}
		return result
	default:
		return input
	}
}

func generatedBindDefinitions(input map[string]template.DefineInput) (*Definitions, map[string]generatedTarget, error) {
	definitions := &Definitions{}

			}

				if err != nil {

			}

				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
			}

				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}

type generatedPrepared struct {
	name        string
	target      string
	assign      Assign
	definitions *Definitions
	rootData    *value.OrderedMap
	env         functions.Env
	runtime     *template.RuntimeEnvironment
	html        *string
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) Render() (string, error) {
	return render.Guard(p.name, p.render)
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) render() (string, error) {
	context := render.NewContext(p.runtime, p.rootData, p.env, p.target)
	runtime := render.NewRuntimeBindings(context)
	scope := render.NewScope()
	if err := context.Enter(p.target, nil, nil); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer context.Leave()
	if p.html != nil {
		if err := context.Write(*p.html, nil, nil); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	} else {
		renderTemplate(p.target, p.assign, p.definitions, context, runtime, p.rootData, scope)
	}
	return context.Output(), nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Prepare(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	name, ok := target.(string)
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("generated target must be a template name")
	}
	return render.Guard(name, func() (template.Prepared, error) {
		return p.prepare(name, assign, options)
	})
}

func generatedBindFailure(name string, err error) error {
	var bindError *value.BindError
	if errors.As(err, &bindError) {
		return errs.WithoutPosition(bindError.Code, name, bindError.Message)
	}
	return err
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) prepare(name string, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	rootData, err := value.BindMap(assign)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	var typedAssign Assign
	if err := generatedDecode(rootData, &typedAssign); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	definitions, targets, err := generatedBindDefinitions(options.Define)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	resolved := targets[name]
	targetName := name
	if resolved.target != "" {
		targetName = resolved.target
	}
	return &generatedPrepared{name: name, target: targetName, assign: typedAssign, definitions: definitions, rootData: rootData, env: generatedEnv(options), runtime: p.Runtime, html: resolved.html}, nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Render(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (string, error) {
	prepared, err := p.Prepare(target, assign, options)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return prepared.Render()

function generatedEnv(input) {
  if (input === void 0 || input === null) return { timezone: "Z", now: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3) };
  if (!generatedPlain(input)) throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env is not an object");
  const timezone = ("timezone" in input ? input.timezone : void 0) ?? "Z";
  const now = ("now" in input ? input.now : void 0) ?? Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3);
  if (typeof timezone !== "string") throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env.timezone is not a string");
  if (typeof now !== "number") throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env.now is not a number");
  return { timezone, now };
}

    this.runtime = runtime;
  }

    const html = registered?.html;
    if (html !== void 0) return new GeneratedPreparedRender(target, () => html);
    const targetName = registered?.target ?? target;

      }

  render(target, assign, options = {}) {
    return this.prepare(target, assign, options).render();
  }

func generatedValue(input any) value.Value {
	switch item := input.(type) {
	case nil:
		return nil
	case bool:
		return item
	case float64:
		return item
	case string:
		return item
	case value.SafeString:
		return item
	case value.List:
		result := make(value.List, len(item))
		for index, element := range item {
			result[index] = generatedValue(element)
		}
		return result
	case *value.OrderedMap:
		return item
	}
	result, err := value.Convert(input)
	generatedPanic(err)
	return result
}

	return result

func generatedWrite(context *render.Context, text string, frame *render.Frame, span ast.Span) {
	generatedPanic(context.Write(text, frame, &span))

func generatedPlain(input any) any {
	switch item := input.(type) {
	case *value.OrderedMap:
		result := map[string]any{}
		for _, key := range item.Keys() {
			entry, _ := item.Get(key)
			result[key] = generatedPlain(entry)
		}

	case value.List:

		for index, entry := range item {
			result[index] = generatedPlain(entry)
		}
		return result
	default:
		return input
	}
}

type generatedTarget struct {
	target string
	html   *string
}

func generatedBindDefinitions(input map[string]template.DefineInput) (*Definitions, map[string]generatedTarget, error) {
	definitions := &Definitions{}
	targets := map[string]generatedTarget{}
	for id, input := range input {
		if !utf8.ValidString(id) {
			return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataInvalidUTF8, Message: "a define id is not valid UTF-8"}
		}
		switch id {
		case "content":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				if input.Template != "" || input.Data != nil {
					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
				}
				definitions.Content = &Definition[struct{}]{HTML: input.HTML}
				targets[id] = generatedTarget{html: input.HTML}
				continue
			}
			if input.Template != "" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[struct{}]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if _, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap); !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := struct{}{}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Content = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: ""}
		case "layout":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
			}
			if input.Template != "layout.tpl" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[DefinitionData_layout_tpl]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if _, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap); !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := DefinitionData_layout_tpl{}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Layout = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: "layout.tpl"}
		default:
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s is not declared", id)
		}
	}
	return definitions, targets, nil
}

func NewGeneratedProgram(options template.Options) (*GeneratedProgram, error) {
	runtime, err := template.NewRuntimeEnvironment(options.Limits, options.Functions)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

type generatedPrepared struct {
	name        string
	target      string
	assign      Assign
	definitions *Definitions
	rootData    *value.OrderedMap
	env         functions.Env
	runtime     *template.RuntimeEnvironment
	html        *string
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) Render() (string, error) {
	return render.Guard(p.name, p.render)
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) render() (string, error) {
	context := render.NewContext(p.runtime, p.rootData, p.env, p.target)
	runtime := render.NewRuntimeBindings(context)
	scope := render.NewScope()
	if err := context.Enter(p.target, nil, nil); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer context.Leave()
	if p.html != nil {
		if err := context.Write(*p.html, nil, nil); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	} else {
		renderTemplate(p.target, p.assign, p.definitions, context, runtime, p.rootData, scope)
	}
	return context.Output(), nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Prepare(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	name, ok := target.(string)
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("generated target must be a template name")
	}
	return render.Guard(name, func() (template.Prepared, error) {
		return p.prepare(name, assign, options)
	})
}

func generatedBindFailure(name string, err error) error {
	var bindError *value.BindError
	if errors.As(err, &bindError) {
		return errs.WithoutPosition(bindError.Code, name, bindError.Message)
	}
	return err
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) prepare(name string, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	rootData, err := value.BindMap(assign)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	var typedAssign Assign
	if err := generatedDecode(rootData, &typedAssign); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	definitions, targets, err := generatedBindDefinitions(options.Define)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	resolved := targets[name]
	targetName := name
	if resolved.target != "" {
		targetName = resolved.target
	}
	return &generatedPrepared{name: name, target: targetName, assign: typedAssign, definitions: definitions, rootData: rootData, env: generatedEnv(options), runtime: p.Runtime, html: resolved.html}, nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Render(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (string, error) {
	prepared, err := p.Prepare(target, assign, options)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return prepared.Render()
}

{
    public static function render(
        string $target,
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,
    ): void {

                    $assign,

                    $rootData,
                    $scope,
                );
                return;
            default:
                throw $context->fail('E_LOAD_NOT_FOUND', null, null, 'template ' . $target . ' does not exist');
        }
    }

    private static function renderLayoutTpl(
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        InputLayoutTpl $input,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,

        $context->at($frame, [0, 29]);

        $context->write($runtime->escape($assign->heading, $frame, [32, 39]));
        $context->at($frame, [40, 46]);
        $context->write(('</h1>' . "\n"));
        $definition = $definitions->content;
        if ($definition?->html === null) {
            throw $runtime->error($frame, [46, 57], 'E_RUNTIME_BLOCK_UNDEFINED', 'define content is not registered');
        }
        $context->at($frame, [46, 57]);
        $context->write($definition->html);
        $context->at($frame, [58, 69]);
        $context->write(('</section>' . "\n"));
    }

function generatedEnv(input) {
  if (input === void 0 || input === null) return { timezone: "Z", now: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3) };
  if (!generatedPlain(input)) throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env is not an object");
  const timezone = ("timezone" in input ? input.timezone : void 0) ?? "Z";
  const now = ("now" in input ? input.now : void 0) ?? Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3);
  if (typeof timezone !== "string") throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env.timezone is not a string");
  if (typeof now !== "number") throw new BindError("E_DATA_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE", "env.now is not a number");
  return { timezone, now };
}

  runtime;
  constructor(runtime = new RuntimeEnvironment()) {
    this.runtime = runtime;
  }

    return internalBoundary(target, () => this.prepareBound(target, assign, options));
  }
  prepareBound(target, assign, options) {

      boundAssign = generatedBindAssign(assign);
      bound = generatedBindDefinitions(options.define);
      env = generatedEnv(options.env);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof BindError) throw new TemplateError({ code: error.code, template: target, line: 0, col: 0, offset: 0, end: 0, message: error.message });
      throw error;
    }
    const registered = bound.targets.get(target);
    const html = registered?.html;
    if (html !== void 0) return new GeneratedPreparedRender(target, () => html);
    const targetName = registered?.target ?? target;
    return new GeneratedPreparedRender(target, () => {
      const context = new RenderContext(this.runtime, boundAssign.root, env, targetName);
      const runtime = new RuntimeBindings(context);
      const scope = new Scope();

      }

    return this.prepare(target, assign, options).render();
  }

func generatedBindDefinitions(input map[string]template.DefineInput) (*Definitions, map[string]generatedTarget, error) {
	definitions := &Definitions{}
	targets := map[string]generatedTarget{}
	for id, input := range input {
		if !utf8.ValidString(id) {
			return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataInvalidUTF8, Message: "a define id is not valid UTF-8"}
		}
		switch id {
		case "content":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				if input.Template != "" || input.Data != nil {
					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
				}
				definitions.Content = &Definition[DefinitionData_content_tpl]{HTML: input.HTML}
				targets[id] = generatedTarget{html: input.HTML}
				continue
			}
			if input.Template != "content.tpl" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[DefinitionData_content_tpl]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if _, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap); !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := DefinitionData_content_tpl{}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Content = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: "content.tpl"}
		case "layout":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
			}
			if input.Template != "layout.tpl" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[DefinitionData_layout_tpl]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if _, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap); !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := DefinitionData_layout_tpl{}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Layout = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: "layout.tpl"}
		default:
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s is not declared", id)
		}
	}
	return definitions, targets, nil
}

type generatedPrepared struct {
	name        string
	target      string
	assign      Assign
	definitions *Definitions
	rootData    *value.OrderedMap
	env         functions.Env
	runtime     *template.RuntimeEnvironment
	html        *string
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) Render() (string, error) {
	return render.Guard(p.name, p.render)
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) render() (string, error) {
	context := render.NewContext(p.runtime, p.rootData, p.env, p.target)
	runtime := render.NewRuntimeBindings(context)
	scope := render.NewScope()
	if err := context.Enter(p.target, nil, nil); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer context.Leave()
	if p.html != nil {
		if err := context.Write(*p.html, nil, nil); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	} else {
		renderTemplate(p.target, p.assign, p.definitions, context, runtime, p.rootData, scope)
	}
	return context.Output(), nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Prepare(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	name, ok := target.(string)
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("generated target must be a template name")
	}
	return render.Guard(name, func() (template.Prepared, error) {
		return p.prepare(name, assign, options)
	})
}

func generatedBindFailure(name string, err error) error {
	var bindError *value.BindError
	if errors.As(err, &bindError) {
		return errs.WithoutPosition(bindError.Code, name, bindError.Message)
	}
	return err
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) prepare(name string, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	rootData, err := value.BindMap(assign)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	var typedAssign Assign
	if err := generatedDecode(rootData, &typedAssign); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	definitions, targets, err := generatedBindDefinitions(options.Define)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	resolved := targets[name]
	targetName := name
	if resolved.target != "" {
		targetName = resolved.target
	}
	return &generatedPrepared{name: name, target: targetName, assign: typedAssign, definitions: definitions, rootData: rootData, env: generatedEnv(options), runtime: p.Runtime, html: resolved.html}, nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Render(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (string, error) {
	prepared, err := p.Prepare(target, assign, options)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return prepared.Render()
}

fn generated_value<T: GeneratedConvert>(input: &T) -> Result<Value, TemplateError> { Ok(input.to_value()) }

fn render_template(target: &str, assign: &Assign, definitions: &mut Definitions, context: &mut RenderContext<'_>, runtime: &RuntimeBindings, root_data: &Rc<RuntimeOrderedMap>, scope: &mut Scope) -> Result<(), TemplateError> { match target {
        "content.tpl" => render_content_tpl(assign, definitions, InputContentTpl::default(), context, runtime, root_data, scope),
        "layout.tpl" => render_layout_tpl(assign, definitions, InputLayoutTpl::default(), context, runtime, root_data, scope),
        _ => Err(context.fail(ErrorCode::E_LOAD_NOT_FOUND, None, None, format!("template {target} does not exist"))),
} }
fn generated_error(message: String) -> RequestError { RequestError::Argument(ArgumentError { message }) }
struct GeneratedTarget { target: Option<String>, html: Option<String> }
fn generated_bind_definitions(name: &str, input: &HashMap<String, polyspec_template::DefineInput>) -> Result<(Definitions, HashMap<String, GeneratedTarget>), RequestError> { let mut definitions = Definitions::default(); let mut targets = HashMap::new(); for (id, input) in input { match id.as_str() {
            "content" => { if let Some(html) = &input.html { if false || input.template.is_some() || input.data.is_some() { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid html entry"))); } definitions.content = Some(Definition { html: Some(html.clone()) }); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: None, html: Some(html.clone()) }); } else { if input.template.as_deref() != Some("content.tpl") { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid template"))); }
if let Some(data) = &input.data { match polyspec_template::bind_data(data).map_err(|error| TemplateError::without_position(error.code, name, error.message))? { Value::Map(_) => {  }, _ => return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data is not an object"))) } }
definitions.content = Some(Definition::default()); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: Some("content.tpl".to_string()), html: None }); } },
            "layout" => { if let Some(html) = &input.html { if true || input.template.is_some() || input.data.is_some() { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid html entry"))); } definitions.layout = Some(Definition { html: Some(html.clone()) }); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: None, html: Some(html.clone()) }); } else { if input.template.as_deref() != Some("layout.tpl") { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid template"))); }
if let Some(data) = &input.data { match polyspec_template::bind_data(data).map_err(|error| TemplateError::without_position(error.code, name, error.message))? { Value::Map(_) => {  }, _ => return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data is not an object"))) } }
definitions.layout = Some(Definition::default()); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: Some("layout.tpl".to_string()), html: None }); } },
            _ => return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} is not declared"))),
        } } Ok((definitions, targets)) }
pub struct GeneratedProgram { pub runtime: RuntimeEnvironment }
impl GeneratedProgram { pub fn new(runtime: RuntimeEnvironment) -> GeneratedProgram { GeneratedProgram { runtime } }  }
impl Program for GeneratedProgram { fn prepare(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &JsonValue, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<PreparedRender<'_>, RequestError> { let RenderTarget::Name(name) = target else { return Err(generated_error("generated target must be a template name".to_string())); }; internal_boundary(name, || { let root_data = Rc::new(polyspec_template::value::bind::bind_map(assign).map_err(|error| TemplateError::without_position(error.code, name, error.message))?); self.prepare_root(name, root_data, options) }) } fn render(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &JsonValue, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<String, RequestError> { Ok(self.prepare(target, assign, options)?.render()?) } fn prepare_bound(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &polyspec_template::BoundMap, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<PreparedRender<'_>, RequestError> { let RenderTarget::Name(name) = target else { return Err(generated_error("generated target must be a template name".to_string())); }; internal_boundary(name, || self.prepare_root(name, polyspec_template::bound_root(assign), options)) } fn render_bound(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &polyspec_template::BoundMap, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<String, RequestError> { Ok(self.prepare_bound(target, assign, options)?.render()?) } }
impl GeneratedProgram { fn prepare_root<'e>(&'e self, name: &str, root_data: Rc<RuntimeOrderedMap>, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<PreparedRender<'e>, RequestError> { let typed_assign: Assign = Assign::from_value(&Value::Map(Rc::clone(&root_data))).map_err(|error| generated_error(error.message))?; let (definitions, targets) = generated_bind_definitions(name, &options.define)?; let resolved = targets.get(name); let target_name = resolved.and_then(|value| value.target.clone()).unwrap_or_else(|| name.to_string()); let html = resolved.and_then(|value| value.html.clone()); let env = options.env.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| polyspec_template::Env { timezone: "Z".to_string(), now: std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).map(|duration| duration.as_secs() as f64).unwrap_or(0.0) }); Ok(PreparedRender::new(name, move || { let mut context = RenderContext::new(&self.runtime, Rc::clone(&root_data), env.clone(), &target_name); let runtime = RuntimeBindings::new(); let mut scope = Scope::default(); let mut render_definitions = definitions.clone(); context.enter(&target_name, None, None)?; let result = if let Some(html) = &html { let frame = Frame { name: target_name.clone(), lines: None, context: Rc::clone(&root_data) }; context.write(html, &frame, [0, 0]) } else { render_template(&target_name, &typed_assign, &mut render_definitions, &mut context, &runtime, &root_data, &mut scope) }; context.leave(); result?; Ok(context.output) })) } }

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Polyspec\Showcase\Generated\ReactBoundary;

use Polyspec\Template\Render\Context;
use Polyspec\Template\Render\Frame;
use Polyspec\Template\Render\RuntimeBindings;

use Polyspec\Template\Value\MapValue;

final class GeneratedTemplates
{
    public static function render(
        string $target,
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,
    ): void {
        switch ($target) {
            case 'content.tpl':
                self::renderContentTpl(
                    $assign,
                    $definitions,
                    new InputContentTpl(),
                    $context,
                    $runtime,
                    $rootData,
                    $scope,
                );
                return;
            case 'layout.tpl':
                self::renderLayoutTpl(
                    $assign,
                    $definitions,
                    new InputLayoutTpl(),
                    $context,
                    $runtime,
                    $rootData,
                    $scope,
                );
                return;
            default:
                throw $context->fail('E_LOAD_NOT_FOUND', null, null, 'template ' . $target . ' does not exist');
        }
    }

    private static function renderContentTpl(
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        InputContentTpl $input,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,
    ): void {
        $frame = new Frame('content.tpl', [0, 41, 65, 76], $rootData);
        $context->at($frame, [0, 44]);
        $context->write(('<section data-react-island id="counter">' . "\n" . '<p>'));
        $context->at($frame, [44, 60]);
        $context->write($runtime->escape($assign->island_label, $frame, [47, 59]));
        $context->at($frame, [60, 76]);
        $context->write(('</p>' . "\n" . '</section>' . "\n"));
    }

    private static function renderLayoutTpl(
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        InputLayoutTpl $input,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,
    ): void {
        $frame = new Frame('layout.tpl', [0, 7, 26, 38, 46], $rootData);
        $context->at($frame, [0, 11]);
        $context->write(('<main>' . "\n" . '<h1>'));
        $context->at($frame, [11, 20]);
        $context->write($runtime->escape($assign->title, $frame, [14, 19]));
        $context->at($frame, [20, 26]);
        $context->write(('</h1>' . "\n"));
        $definition = $definitions->content;
        if ($definition === null) {
            throw $runtime->error($frame, [26, 37], 'E_RUNTIME_BLOCK_UNDEFINED', 'define content is not registered');
        }
        if ($definition?->html !== null) {
            $context->at($frame, [26, 37]);
            $context->write($definition->html);
        } else {
            if ($definition?->data !== null && !($definition->data instanceof DefinitionDataContentTpl)) {
                throw $runtime->error(
                    $frame,
                    [26, 37],
                    'E_RUNTIME_TYPE',
                    'generated definition content data has an invalid type',
                );

            $input = new InputContentTpl();
            $blockScope = new Scope();

            try {
                self::renderContentTpl($assign, $definitions, $input, $context, $runtime, $rootData, $blockScope);
            } finally {
                $context->leave();
            }
        }
        $context->at($frame, [38, 46]);
        $context->write(('</main>' . "\n"));
    }

  page: Page;

	Defined_label string  `json:"defined_label"`
	Layout_local  *string `json:"layout_local"`
}

type Input_layout_tpl struct{}

type DefinitionData_content_tpl struct {
	Title         *string  `json:"title"`
	Root_label    *string  `json:"root_label"`
	Defined_label *string  `json:"defined_label"`
	Layout_local  **string `json:"layout_local"`
}

type DefinitionData_layout_tpl struct{}

type Definition[T any] struct {
	Template string  `json:"template"`
	HTML     *string `json:"html"`
	Data     *T      `json:"data"`
}

type Definitions struct {
	Content *Definition[DefinitionData_content_tpl] `json:"content"`
	Layout  *Definition[DefinitionData_layout_tpl]  `json:"layout"`
}

type ArtifactManifest struct {
	Schema         int
	Mode           string
	Target         string
	Entry          string
	SourceDigest   string
	TypeDigest     string
	ContractDigest string
	Files          map[string]string
}

func generatedPanic(err error) {
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
}

func generatedValue(input any) value.Value {
	switch item := input.(type) {
	case nil:
		return nil
	case bool:
		return item
	case float64:
		return item
	case string:
		return item
	case value.SafeString:
		return item
	case value.List:
		result := make(value.List, len(item))
		for index, element := range item {
			result[index] = generatedValue(element)
		}
		return result
	case *value.OrderedMap:
		return item
	}
	result, err := value.Convert(input)
	generatedPanic(err)
	return result
}

func generatedResult[T any](input value.Value) T {
	result, err := generatedAs[T](input)
	generatedPanic(err)
	return result
}

func generatedAs[T any](input value.Value) (T, error) {
	if result, ok := input.(T); ok {
		return result, nil
	}
	var result T
	err := generatedDecode(input, &result)
	return result, err
}

func generatedCall[T any](runtime *render.RuntimeBindings, name string, args []value.Value, frame *render.Frame, span ast.Span) T {
	result, err := runtime.Call(name, args, frame, span)
	generatedPanic(err)
	return generatedResult[T](result)
}

func generatedEscape(runtime *render.RuntimeBindings, input any, frame *render.Frame, span ast.Span) string {
	result, err := runtime.Escape(generatedValue(input), frame, span)
	generatedPanic(err)
	return result
}

func generatedWrite(context *render.Context, text string, frame *render.Frame, span ast.Span) {
	generatedPanic(context.Write(text, frame, &span))
}

func generatedEnter(context *render.Context, name string, frame *render.Frame, span ast.Span) {
	generatedPanic(context.Enter(name, frame, &span))
}

func generatedPlain(input any) any {
	switch item := input.(type) {
	case *value.OrderedMap:
		result := map[string]any{}
		for _, key := range item.Keys() {
			entry, _ := item.Get(key)
			result[key] = generatedPlain(entry)
		}
		return result
	case value.List:
		result := make([]any, len(item))
		for index, entry := range item {
			result[index] = generatedPlain(entry)
		}
		return result
	default:
		return input
	}
}

func generatedDecode(input any, output any) error {
	data, err := json.Marshal(generatedPlain(input))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return json.Unmarshal(data, output)
}

func generatedEnv(options template.RenderOptions) functions.Env {
	env := functions.Env{Timezone: "Z", Now: float64(time.Now().Unix())}
	if options.Env != nil {
		if options.Env.Timezone != "" {
			env.Timezone = options.Env.Timezone
		}
		env.Now = options.Env.Now
	}
	return env
}

func render_content_tpl(assign Assign, definitions *Definitions, input Input_content_tpl, context *render.Context, runtime *render.RuntimeBindings, rootData *value.OrderedMap, scope *render.Scope) {
	frame := render.NewFrame("content.tpl", errs.LineIndex{0, 10, 29, 64, 105, 164, 175}, rootData)
	scope.Locals["title"] = generatedValue(input.Title)
	scope.Locals["root_label"] = generatedValue(input.Root_label)
	scope.Locals["defined_label"] = generatedValue(input.Defined_label)
	scope.Locals["layout_local"] = generatedValue(input.Layout_local)
	generatedWrite(context, "<article>\u000a<h1>", frame, ast.Span{0, 14})
	generatedWrite(context, generatedEscape(runtime, input.Title, frame, ast.Span{17, 22}), frame, ast.Span{14, 23})
	generatedWrite(context, "</h1>\u000a<p class=\"root\">", frame, ast.Span{23, 45})
	generatedWrite(context, generatedEscape(runtime, input.Root_label, frame, ast.Span{48, 58}), frame, ast.Span{45, 59})
	generatedWrite(context, "</p>\u000a<p class=\"defined\">", frame, ast.Span{59, 83})
	generatedWrite(context, generatedEscape(runtime, input.Defined_label, frame, ast.Span{86, 99}), frame, ast.Span{83, 100})
	generatedWrite(context, "</p>\u000a<p class=\"local\">", frame, ast.Span{100, 122})
	generatedWrite(context, generatedEscape(runtime, generatedCall[string](runtime, "default", []value.Value{generatedValue(input.Layout_local), generatedValue("missing")}, frame, ast.Span{125, 158}), frame, ast.Span{125, 158}), frame, ast.Span{122, 159})
	generatedWrite(context, "</p>\u000a</article>\u000a", frame, ast.Span{159, 175})
}

func render_layout_tpl(assign Assign, definitions *Definitions, input Input_layout_tpl, context *render.Context, runtime *render.RuntimeBindings, rootData *value.OrderedMap, scope *render.Scope) {
	frame := render.NewFrame("layout.tpl", errs.LineIndex{0, 44, 68, 97, 108}, rootData)
	var local_layout_local_1 string = "visible only in layout"
	scope.Locals["layout_local"] = generatedValue(local_layout_local_1)
	generatedWrite(context, "<section class=\"scope\">\u000a", frame, ast.Span{44, 68})
	{
		definition := definitions.Content
		if definition == nil {
			panic(runtime.Error(frame, ast.Span{68, 96}, errs.RuntimeBlockUndefined, "define content is not registered"))
		}
		if definition != nil && definition.HTML != nil {
			generatedWrite(context, *definition.HTML, frame, ast.Span{68, 96})
		} else {
			input := Input_content_tpl{Root_label: assign.Root_label, Defined_label: assign.Defined_label}
			if definition != nil && definition.Data != nil {
				if definition.Data.Title != nil {
					input.Title = *definition.Data.Title
				}
				if definition.Data.Root_label != nil {
					input.Root_label = *definition.Data.Root_label
				}
				if definition.Data.Defined_label != nil {
					input.Defined_label = *definition.Data.Defined_label
				}
				if definition.Data.Layout_local != nil {
					input.Layout_local = *definition.Data.Layout_local
				}
			}
			input.Title = assign.Page.Title
			blockScope := render.NewScope()
			generatedEnter(context, "content.tpl", frame, ast.Span{68, 96})
			func() {
				defer context.Leave()
				render_content_tpl(assign, definitions, input, context, runtime, rootData, blockScope)
			}()
		}
	}
	generatedWrite(context, "</section>\u000a", frame, ast.Span{97, 108})
}

func renderTemplate(target string, assign Assign, definitions *Definitions, context *render.Context, runtime *render.RuntimeBindings, rootData *value.OrderedMap, scope *render.Scope) {
	switch target {
	case "layout.tpl":
		render_layout_tpl(assign, definitions, Input_layout_tpl{}, context, runtime, rootData, scope)
		return
	default:
		panic(context.Fail(errs.LoadNotFound, nil, nil, "template "+target+" does not exist"))
	}
}

type generatedTarget struct {
	target string
	html   *string
}

func generatedBindDefinitions(input map[string]template.DefineInput) (*Definitions, map[string]generatedTarget, error) {
	definitions := &Definitions{}
	targets := map[string]generatedTarget{}
	for id, input := range input {
		if !utf8.ValidString(id) {
			return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataInvalidUTF8, Message: "a define id is not valid UTF-8"}
		}
		switch id {
		case "content":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				if input.Template != "" || input.Data != nil {
					return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
				}
				definitions.Content = &Definition[DefinitionData_content_tpl]{HTML: input.HTML}
				targets[id] = generatedTarget{html: input.HTML}
				continue
			}
			if input.Template != "content.tpl" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[DefinitionData_content_tpl]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				data, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap)
				if !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := DefinitionData_content_tpl{}
				if raw, found := data.Get("title"); found {
					converted, err := generatedAs[string](raw)
					if err != nil {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s.data.%s: %w", id, "title", err)
					}
					definitionData.Title = &converted
				}
				if raw, found := data.Get("root_label"); found {
					converted, err := generatedAs[string](raw)
					if err != nil {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s.data.%s: %w", id, "root_label", err)
					}
					definitionData.Root_label = &converted
				}
				if raw, found := data.Get("defined_label"); found {
					converted, err := generatedAs[string](raw)
					if err != nil {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s.data.%s: %w", id, "defined_label", err)
					}
					definitionData.Defined_label = &converted
				}
				if raw, found := data.Get("layout_local"); found {
					converted, err := generatedAs[*string](raw)
					if err != nil {
						return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s.data.%s: %w", id, "layout_local", err)
					}
					definitionData.Layout_local = &converted
				}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Content = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: "content.tpl"}
		case "layout":
			if input.HTML != nil {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid html entry", id)
			}
			if input.Template != "layout.tpl" {
				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s has an invalid template", id)
			}
			entry := &Definition[DefinitionData_layout_tpl]{Template: input.Template}
			if input.Data != nil {
				bound, err := value.BindData(input.Data)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, nil, err
				}
				if _, ok := bound.(*value.OrderedMap); !ok {
					return nil, nil, &value.BindError{Code: errs.DataUnsupportedType, Message: "define " + id + ": data is not a map"}
				}
				definitionData := DefinitionData_layout_tpl{}
				entry.Data = &definitionData
			}
			definitions.Layout = entry
			targets[id] = generatedTarget{target: "layout.tpl"}
		default:
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("define.%s is not declared", id)
		}
	}
	return definitions, targets, nil
}

type GeneratedProgram struct {
	Runtime *template.RuntimeEnvironment
}

func NewGeneratedProgram(options template.Options) (*GeneratedProgram, error) {
	runtime, err := template.NewRuntimeEnvironment(options.Limits, options.Functions)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &GeneratedProgram{Runtime: runtime}, nil
}

type generatedPrepared struct {
	name        string
	target      string
	assign      Assign
	definitions *Definitions
	rootData    *value.OrderedMap
	env         functions.Env
	runtime     *template.RuntimeEnvironment
	html        *string
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) Render() (string, error) {
	return render.Guard(p.name, p.render)
}

func (p *generatedPrepared) render() (string, error) {
	context := render.NewContext(p.runtime, p.rootData, p.env, p.target)
	runtime := render.NewRuntimeBindings(context)
	scope := render.NewScope()
	if err := context.Enter(p.target, nil, nil); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer context.Leave()
	if p.html != nil {
		if err := context.Write(*p.html, nil, nil); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	} else {
		renderTemplate(p.target, p.assign, p.definitions, context, runtime, p.rootData, scope)
	}
	return context.Output(), nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Prepare(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	name, ok := target.(string)
	if !ok {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("generated target must be a template name")
	}
	return render.Guard(name, func() (template.Prepared, error) {
		return p.prepare(name, assign, options)
	})
}

func generatedBindFailure(name string, err error) error {
	var bindError *value.BindError
	if errors.As(err, &bindError) {
		return errs.WithoutPosition(bindError.Code, name, bindError.Message)
	}
	return err
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) prepare(name string, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (template.Prepared, error) {
	rootData, err := value.BindMap(assign)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	var typedAssign Assign
	if err := generatedDecode(rootData, &typedAssign); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	definitions, targets, err := generatedBindDefinitions(options.Define)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, generatedBindFailure(name, err)
	}
	resolved := targets[name]
	targetName := name
	if resolved.target != "" {
		targetName = resolved.target
	}
	return &generatedPrepared{name: name, target: targetName, assign: typedAssign, definitions: definitions, rootData: rootData, env: generatedEnv(options), runtime: p.Runtime, html: resolved.html}, nil
}

func (p *GeneratedProgram) Render(target any, assign any, options template.RenderOptions) (string, error) {
	prepared, err := p.Prepare(target, assign, options)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return prepared.Render()
}

var _ template.Program = (*GeneratedProgram)(nil)

} }
fn generated_error(message: String) -> RequestError { RequestError::Argument(ArgumentError { message }) }
struct GeneratedTarget { target: Option<String>, html: Option<String> }
fn generated_bind_definitions(name: &str, input: &HashMap<String, polyspec_template::DefineInput>) -> Result<(Definitions, HashMap<String, GeneratedTarget>), RequestError> { let mut definitions = Definitions::default(); let mut targets = HashMap::new(); for (id, input) in input { match id.as_str() {
            "content" => { if let Some(html) = &input.html { if false || input.template.is_some() || input.data.is_some() { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid html entry"))); } definitions.content = Some(Definition { html: Some(html.clone()), ..Default::default() }); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: None, html: Some(html.clone()) }); } else { if input.template.as_deref() != Some("content.tpl") { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid template"))); }
let data = match &input.data { None => None, Some(data) => match polyspec_template::bind_data(data).map_err(|error| TemplateError::without_position(error.code, name, error.message))? { Value::Map(map) => { let _ = &map; Some(DefinitionDataContentTpl { title: map.get("title").map(GeneratedConvert::from_value).transpose().map_err(|error| generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data.title: {}", error.message)))?, root_label: map.get("root_label").map(GeneratedConvert::from_value).transpose().map_err(|error| generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data.root_label: {}", error.message)))?, defined_label: map.get("defined_label").map(GeneratedConvert::from_value).transpose().map_err(|error| generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data.defined_label: {}", error.message)))?, layout_local: map.get("layout_local").map(GeneratedConvert::from_value).transpose().map_err(|error| generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data.layout_local: {}", error.message)))? }) }, _ => return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data is not an object"))) } };
definitions.content = Some(Definition { data, ..Default::default() }); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: Some("content.tpl".to_string()), html: None }); } },
            "layout" => { if let Some(html) = &input.html { if true || input.template.is_some() || input.data.is_some() { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid html entry"))); } definitions.layout = Some(Definition { html: Some(html.clone()), ..Default::default() }); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: None, html: Some(html.clone()) }); } else { if input.template.as_deref() != Some("layout.tpl") { return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} has an invalid template"))); }
let data = match &input.data { None => None, Some(data) => match polyspec_template::bind_data(data).map_err(|error| TemplateError::without_position(error.code, name, error.message))? { Value::Map(map) => { let _ = &map; Some(DefinitionDataLayoutTpl {  }) }, _ => return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id}.data is not an object"))) } };
definitions.layout = Some(Definition { data, ..Default::default() }); targets.insert(id.clone(), GeneratedTarget { target: Some("layout.tpl".to_string()), html: None }); } },
            _ => return Err(generated_error(format!("define.{id} is not declared"))),
        } } Ok((definitions, targets)) }
pub struct GeneratedProgram { pub runtime: RuntimeEnvironment }
impl GeneratedProgram { pub fn new(runtime: RuntimeEnvironment) -> GeneratedProgram { GeneratedProgram { runtime } }  }
impl Program for GeneratedProgram { fn prepare(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &JsonValue, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<PreparedRender<'_>, RequestError> { let RenderTarget::Name(name) = target else { return Err(generated_error("generated target must be a template name".to_string())); }; internal_boundary(name, || { let root_data = Rc::new(polyspec_template::value::bind::bind_map(assign).map_err(|error| TemplateError::without_position(error.code, name, error.message))?); self.prepare_root(name, root_data, options) }) } fn render(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &JsonValue, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<String, RequestError> { Ok(self.prepare(target, assign, options)?.render()?) } fn prepare_bound(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &polyspec_template::BoundMap, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<PreparedRender<'_>, RequestError> { let RenderTarget::Name(name) = target else { return Err(generated_error("generated target must be a template name".to_string())); }; internal_boundary(name, || self.prepare_root(name, polyspec_template::bound_root(assign), options)) } fn render_bound(&self, target: RenderTarget<'_>, assign: &polyspec_template::BoundMap, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<String, RequestError> { Ok(self.prepare_bound(target, assign, options)?.render()?) } }
impl GeneratedProgram { fn prepare_root<'e>(&'e self, name: &str, root_data: Rc<RuntimeOrderedMap>, options: &RenderOptions) -> Result<PreparedRender<'e>, RequestError> { let typed_assign: Assign = Assign::from_value(&Value::Map(Rc::clone(&root_data))).map_err(|error| generated_error(error.message))?; let (definitions, targets) = generated_bind_definitions(name, &options.define)?; let resolved = targets.get(name); let target_name = resolved.and_then(|value| value.target.clone()).unwrap_or_else(|| name.to_string()); let html = resolved.and_then(|value| value.html.clone()); let env = options.env.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| polyspec_template::Env { timezone: "Z".to_string(), now: std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).map(|duration| duration.as_secs() as f64).unwrap_or(0.0) }); Ok(PreparedRender::new(name, move || { let mut context = RenderContext::new(&self.runtime, Rc::clone(&root_data), env.clone(), &target_name); let runtime = RuntimeBindings::new(); let mut scope = Scope::default(); let mut render_definitions = definitions.clone(); context.enter(&target_name, None, None)?; let result = if let Some(html) = &html { let frame = Frame { name: target_name.clone(), lines: None, context: Rc::clone(&root_data) }; context.write(html, &frame, [0, 0]) } else { render_template(&target_name, &typed_assign, &mut render_definitions, &mut context, &runtime, &root_data, &mut scope) }; context.leave(); result?; Ok(context.output) })) } }

<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Polyspec\Showcase\Generated\ScopePrecedence;

use Polyspec\Template\Render\Context;
use Polyspec\Template\Render\Frame;
use Polyspec\Template\Render\RuntimeBindings;
use Polyspec\Template\Render\Scope;
use Polyspec\Template\Value\MapValue;

final class GeneratedTemplates
{
    public static function render(
        string $target,
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,
    ): void {
        switch ($target) {
            case 'layout.tpl':
                self::renderLayoutTpl(
                    $assign,
                    $definitions,
                    new InputLayoutTpl(),
                    $context,
                    $runtime,
                    $rootData,
                    $scope,
                );
                return;
            default:
                throw $context->fail('E_LOAD_NOT_FOUND', null, null, 'template ' . $target . ' does not exist');
        }
    }

    private static function renderContentTpl(
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        InputContentTpl $input,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,
    ): void {
        $frame = new Frame('content.tpl', [0, 10, 29, 64, 105, 164, 175], $rootData);
        $scope->locals->set('title', $input->title);
        $scope->locals->set('root_label', $input->root_label);
        $scope->locals->set('defined_label', $input->defined_label);
        $scope->locals->set('layout_local', $input->layout_local);
        $context->at($frame, [0, 14]);
        $context->write(('<article>' . "\n" . '<h1>'));
        $context->at($frame, [14, 23]);
        $context->write($runtime->escape($scope->lookup($frame, 'title'), $frame, [17, 22]));
        $context->at($frame, [23, 45]);
        $context->write(('</h1>' . "\n" . '<p class="root">'));
        $context->at($frame, [45, 59]);
        $context->write($runtime->escape($scope->lookup($frame, 'root_label'), $frame, [48, 58]));
        $context->at($frame, [59, 83]);
        $context->write(('</p>' . "\n" . '<p class="defined">'));
        $context->at($frame, [83, 100]);
        $context->write($runtime->escape($scope->lookup($frame, 'defined_label'), $frame, [86, 99]));
        $context->at($frame, [100, 122]);
        $context->write(('</p>' . "\n" . '<p class="local">'));
        $context->at($frame, [122, 159]);
        $context->write(
            $runtime->escape(
                $runtime->call('default', [$scope->lookup($frame, 'layout_local'), 'missing'], $frame, [125, 158]),
                $frame,
                [125, 158],
            ),
        );
        $context->at($frame, [159, 175]);
        $context->write(('</p>' . "\n" . '</article>' . "\n"));
    }

    private static function renderLayoutTpl(
        Assign $assign,
        Definitions $definitions,
        InputLayoutTpl $input,
        Context $context,
        RuntimeBindings $runtime,
        MapValue $rootData,
        Scope $scope,
    ): void {
        $frame = new Frame('layout.tpl', [0, 44, 68, 97, 108], $rootData);
        $scope->locals->set('layout_local', 'visible only in layout');
        $context->at($frame, [44, 68]);
        $context->write(('<section class="scope">' . "\n"));
        $definition = $definitions->content;
        if ($definition === null) {
            throw $runtime->error($frame, [68, 96], 'E_RUNTIME_BLOCK_UNDEFINED', 'define content is not registered');
        }
        if ($definition?->html !== null) {
            $context->at($frame, [68, 96]);
            $context->write($definition->html);
        } else {
            if ($definition?->data !== null && !($definition->data instanceof DefinitionDataContentTpl)) {
                throw $runtime->error(
                    $frame,
                    [68, 96],
                    'E_RUNTIME_TYPE',
                    'generated definition content data has an invalid type',
                );
            }
            $input = new InputContentTpl(
                title: ($definition?->data !== null && $definition->data->has_title
                    ? $definition->data->title
                    : throw $runtime->error(
                        $frame,
                        [68, 96],
                        'E_RUNTIME_TYPE',
                        'generated input content.tpl.title is missing',
                    )),
                root_label: $assign->root_label,
                defined_label: $assign->defined_label,
                layout_local: null,
            );
            if ($definition?->data !== null) {
                if ($definition->data->has_title) {
                    $input->title = $definition->data->title;
                }
                if ($definition->data->has_root_label) {
                    $input->root_label = $definition->data->root_label;
                }
                if ($definition->data->has_defined_label) {
                    $input->defined_label = $definition->data->defined_label;
                }
                if ($definition->data->has_layout_local) {
                    $input->layout_local = $definition->data->layout_local;
                }
            }
            $input->title = $assign->page?->title;
            $blockScope = new Scope();
            $context->enter('content.tpl', $frame, [68, 96]);
            try {
                self::renderContentTpl($assign, $definitions, $input, $context, $runtime, $rootData, $blockScope);
            } finally {
                $context->leave();
            }
        }
        $context->at($frame, [97, 108]);
        $context->write(('</section>' . "\n"));
    }
}
